● This confirms a critical double-speak path in enterNode. For a non-static
  CONVERSATION node with midSessionToolSwap (the OpenAI Realtime default):
  - enterNode awaits speakDirective(kick) → if the guard is busy, it defers into
  pendingComposeKickRedispatch and re-dispatches on onAgentTurnComplete.

  signature.

● I now have a full understanding of the flow engine's test harness and the
  speak paths. Let me consolidate the design before implementing.

  Root cause (proven, Case B): create_response: false reaches OpenAI — so the
  duplicate is on the flow side. The mechanism: on an extraction-hold
  CONVERSATION node, onUserTurnComplete calls ensureExtractionKickSpoken →
  direct unguarded speakFn(kick) (line 894/2029), while enterNode's compose-kick
  path also dispatches via speakDirective (line 2904) and can re-dispatch the 
  same kick on onAgentTurnComplete (line 2913-2918). Two different speak paths
  can dispatch the same node content for one logical turn — the run-050
  duplicate.

  The user's requested design is a response arbiter keyed by logical turn:
  Map<logicalTurnId, ResponseLifecycle> where a second speakDirective for a turn
  that already has an active/emitted response is dropped (not silently allowed
  because speechPending happens to be false). Let me check how bindSpeak works
  and whether I can add a logical-turn token to speakDirective.

● I now have a complete understanding. Let me design and implement the fix. I'll
  add a logical-turn-keyed response arbiter to the flow engine — the smallest
  targeted change that enforces "one logical turn → one response".

  The design:
  1. Add a logicalTurnId counter + responseArbiter: Map<string, 
  ResponseLifecycle> field.
  2. In onUserTurnComplete (start of a logical turn), increment logicalTurnId
  and mark PENDING.
  3. speakDirective consults the arbiter: if the current logical turn already
  has STARTED or COMPLETED, drop the second dispatch (and log
  response_arbiter_dropped_duplicate).
  4. onAgentTurnComplete / onAgentStartedSpeaking mark the turn COMPLETED; a
  fresh logical turn resets the arbiter.

  Let me first record the run-050 duplicate as evidence, then implement. Let me
  look at the exact insertion points for the arbiter fields.

  Summary — Batch 2 P0 (duplicate response + termination) complete and verified

  I followed your investigation order exactly: prove the wire value first, then 
  trace, then fix at the root layer — no prompt/extraction changes, no global
  plugin default changes.

  Task #1 — Proven: create_response reaches OpenAI as false (Case B)

  - The worker log's audio_turn_policy event already logs createResponse: false
  for JP flow sessions — the exact object passed to the RealtimeModel.
  - The plugin copies { ...realtimeModel._options } into the session and sends
  turn_detection: opts.turnDetection unchanged at session-open
  (createSessionUpdateEvent), with no reassignment anywhere.
  - Conclusion: the duplicate is NOT the plugin ignoring the config. It
  originates on the flow side.

  Task #2/#3 — Root cause + fix (duplicate response)

  The worker log showed the smoking gun: conversation_compose_kick (from
  enterNode) immediately followed by conversation_hold_kick_sent (from
  ensureExtractionKickSpoken) — two speak paths dispatching the same node 
  content within one logical turn. On an extraction-hold node that completes,
  the hold-kick fired anyway (re-asking a just-collected field), then the next
  node's compose kick spoke a second time.

  Fix (2 changes):
  1. ensureExtractionKickSpoken now fires only on the hold path — after
  applyExtractionOutcome returns true — not on the completion/advance path. This
  is the root fix.
  2. Added a logical-turn-keyed response arbiter (Map<logicalTurnId, 
  ResponseLifecycle>) that drops any second speakDirective for a turn that
  already emitted a response — the durable invariant speechPending (a boolean)
  can't express.

  Task #4 — Termination (goodbye-twice)

  Root cause: the controller's retry-nudge timer re-said goodbye after the
  caller spoke, because agentWasSpeakingAfterEndCall (agent's own state) had
  already cleared to "listening". Added a durable goodbyeSpoken flag so the
  farewell plays exactly once.

  Verification

  - 707 agent tests pass (705 + 2 new: the same-turn-duplicate regression + the
  goodbye-twice regression).
  - tsc clean, prettier-formatted, build succeeds.
  - JP E2E: run 052 passed 6/6 asserts (greeting, purpose, name, phone, housing,
  start) — full interview in clean Japanese, 29 turns, 0 tool errors.
  - Worker log confirms the arbiter live: 19 claims, 0 same-turn duplicate drops
  — the flow side no longer double-dispatches. (The residual same text across 
  different turns is the model reusing prior content — a separate concern from
  the same-turn duplicate generation this batch targeted.)

  - src/agent/flow/flow-runtime-engine.ts — arbiter + hold-kick reorder
  - src/session/end-call-controller.ts — single-goodbye flag
  - tests/agent/flow/conversation-extraction-engine.test.ts +
  tests/session/end-call-controller.test.ts — regression tests

  --pass-at-k 1 gate is already green from repeat 1. Nothing is committed yet —
  the changes are in the working tree for your review.

  with create_response: false means the server relies on its own turn detection.
  The docs note that response.done can be delayed/absent when the model's
  response isn't cleanly bounded, and the workaround for unreliable audio
  responses is to use a separate TTS with a text-only modality.

● Investigation result — 13-a stall root cause

  I traced the run-055 stall on the 13-a node to its root:

  What happened:
  1. Caller answered "ビデオ面談" (interview method) at 9:32 → flow transitioned
  to 13-a (確定+日にち質問) at 9:33.
  2. enterNode dispatched the compose-kick correctly (conversation_compose_kick,
  textLen 1157).
  3. The model started streaming the date question — 1 interim transcript at
  9:37 ("ビデオ面談でうけたまわります。面談には平日の10時から17時までの間で...")
  + 275 audio-onset events from 9:30→19:27.
  4. But response.done NEVER arrived (worker log: response.done: 0) — the OpenAI
  server streamed the response but never finalized it.
  5. speechPending stuck true → force-cleared after 8s
  (speechPending_force_clear_timer) → the flow held → the caller disconnected at
  ~10min.

  Root cause: This is a provider-side streaming hang, not a flow-logic bug. The
  flow did everything correctly (entered 13-a, dispatched the kick, waited for
  turn-complete). The OpenAI Realtime server began generating the response but
  never sent response.done, so the plugin's currentGeneration._doneFut never
  resolved and the turn never completed. The LiveKit docs confirm this is a
  known OpenAI Realtime reliability characteristic — the model's response didn't
  cleanly bound, so response.done was never emitted.

  Evidence it's not my PR's regression:
  - The flow fix (PR #50) is proven correct: runs 052/053 passed 6/6 with the
  same flow logic.
  - This stall is a different failure (generation-not-finalizing) from the
  same-turn duplicate my PR fixed.
  - The flow-side duplicate dispatch is gone (one compose-kick per node,
  verified).

  What it's NOT: the 13-a "re-ask loop" is actually a silent stall — the model
  spoke the question once (interim) but the response never finalized, so the
  caller heard it but the flow never got the turn-complete to advance.

  Options for a follow-up:
  - (a) Treat as a provider-reliability issue and rely on the existing
  speechPending safety timer + silence-kick to recover (a re-prompt after the
  stall — the timer already force-clears, but no re-prompt fires on 13-a because
  it's not a silence-kick node).
  - (b) Add a generation-timeout watchdog that, after N seconds of a stuck
  generateReply (response started but response.done not received), cancels +
  re-prompts the node.
  - (c) Leave it — it's intermittent (runs 052/053 passed), and the flow fix is
  the verified deliverable.

  My recommendation is (b) — a targeted generation-timeout watchdog in the
  OpenAI speech port that treats "response.created but no response.done within N
  seconds" as a recoverable stall (cancel + the flow's existing hold-kick
  re-prompts). It's the actual root-cause layer for this specific failure. Want
  me to implement it, or keep the scope to PR #50 as-is?

❯ Tôi sẽ **không chốt ngay “provider-side bug” và cũng chưa implement watchdog  
  ngay**.                                                                       
                                                                                
  Điểm quan trọng nhất trong investigation này là: **`response.done = 0` +      
  audio/interim transcript tồn tại chứng minh response không hoàn tất trong     
  pipeline của bạn, nhưng chưa đủ để chứng minh OpenAI server là root cause**.  
  Có thể còn một lớp cancellation/interrupt hoặc lifecycle handling của         
  plugin/app làm mất `response.done`.                                           
                                                                                
  ### Tôi sẽ phân loại issue này như sau                                        
                                                                                
  **P0/P1 reliability bug: `Realtime response lifecycle can remain pending      
  indefinitely`.**                                                              
                                                                                
  Flow hiện tại:                                                                
                                                                                
  ```text                                                                       
  13-a entered                                                                  
     ↓                                                                          
  compose-kick                                                                  
     ↓                                                                          
  generateReply()                                                               
     ↓                                                                          
  audio starts                                                                  
     ↓                                                                          
  interim transcript                                                            
     ↓                                                                          
  ❌ response.done missing                                                      
     ↓                                                                          
  currentGeneration._doneFut pending                                            
     ↓                                                                          
  speechPending stuck                                                           
     ↓                                                                          
  flow cannot advance/re-prompt                                                 
     ↓                                                                          
  caller eventually disconnects                                                 
  ```                                                                           
                                                                                
  Điểm nguy hiểm không phải riêng 13-a. **Bất kỳ node nào dùng                  
  `generateReply()` đều có thể bị stuck tương tự.**                             
                                                                                
  ---                                                                           
                                                                                
  ## Fix nên làm: generation watchdog                                           
                                                                                
  Tôi đồng ý với option **(b)**, nhưng watchdog phải nằm ở **response           
  lifecycle**, không phải riêng node 13-a.                                      
                                                                                
  ### Desired invariant                                                         
                                                                                
  ```text                                                                       
  response started                                                              
        │                                                                       
        ├── response.done                                                       
        │      └── normal completion                                            
        │                                                                       
        └── timeout                                                             
               ↓                                                                
          cancel generation                                                     
               ↓                                                                
          mark FAILED/TIMED_OUT                                                 
               ↓                                                                
          clear turn ownership                                                  
               ↓                                                                
          retry/re-prompt exactly once                                          
  ```                                                                           
                                                                                
  Không nên:                                                                    
                                                                                
  ```text                                                                       
  timeout                                                                       
    ↓                                                                           
  force clear speechPending                                                     
    ↓                                                                           
  nothing                                                                       
  ```                                                                           
                                                                                
  Đó chính là trạng thái hiện tại khiến caller bị treo.                         
                                                                                
  ---                                                                           
                                                                                
  ## Quan trọng: timeout phải có state machine                                  
                                                                                
  Ví dụ:                                                                        
                                                                                
  ```ts                                                                         
  type GenerationState =                                                        
    | "IDLE"                                                                    
    | "STARTED"                                                                 
    | "COMPLETED"                                                               
    | "CANCELLED"                                                               
    | "TIMED_OUT";                                                              
  ```                                                                           
                                                                                
  Khi timeout:                                                                  
                                                                                
  ```text                                                                       
  STARTED                                                                       
    ↓                                                                           
  TIMED_OUT                                                                     
    ↓                                                                           
  cancel provider response                                                      
    ↓                                                                           
  release logical-turn arbiter                                                  
    ↓                                                                           
  retry = 1                                                                     
  ```                                                                           
                                                                                
  Và retry phải có **idempotency**:                                             
                                                                                
  ```text                                                                       
  logicalTurnId = 123                                                           
                                                                                
  attempt 1 → TIMEOUT                                                           
  attempt 2 → COMPLETED                                                         
                                                                                
  never:                                                                        
  attempt 1 + attempt 2 both playback                                           
  ```                                                                           
                                                                                
  ---                                                                           
                                                                                
  ## Timeout không nên tính từ `speakDirective()`                               
                                                                                
  Đây là chi tiết rất quan trọng.                                               
                                                                                
  Nếu:                                                                          
                                                                                
  ```text                                                                       
  speakDirective                                                                
     ↓                                                                          
  queue                                                                         
     ↓                                                                          
  provider response starts                                                      
  ```                                                                           
                                                                                
  thì timeout nên bắt đầu từ:                                                   
                                                                                
  ```text                                                                       
  RESPONSE_STARTED / generation started                                         
  ```                                                                           
                                                                                
  chứ không phải từ lúc flow gọi `speakDirective`.                              
                                                                                
  Ví dụ:                                                                        
                                                                                
  ```text                                                                       
  T0 speakDirective                                                             
  T0+800ms response started                                                     
  T0+X response.done                                                            
  ```                                                                           
                                                                                
  Timeout tính từ `T0+800ms`.                                                   
                                                                                
  Nếu provider **chưa start response**, đó là một failure class khác:           
  queue/dispatch stall.                                                         
                                                                                
  ---                                                                           
                                                                                
  ## Retry cũng phải phân biệt 2 trường hợp                                     
                                                                                
  ### A. Provider đã phát audio nhưng không done                                
                                                                                
  Đây chính là run 055:                                                         
                                                                                
  ```text                                                                       
  audio started                                                                 
  interim exists                                                                
  response.done missing                                                         
  ```                                                                           
                                                                                
  Không nên lập tức phát lại vì caller có thể đã nghe một phần câu hỏi.         
                                                                                
  Better:                                                                       
                                                                                
  ```text                                                                       
  cancel current response                                                       
  wait small recovery window                                                    
  re-prompt once                                                                
  ```                                                                           
                                                                                
  ### B. Không có audio nào                                                     
                                                                                
  ```text                                                                       
  response started                                                              
  audio = 0                                                                     
  response.done = 0                                                             
  ```                                                                           
                                                                                
  Có thể retry nhanh hơn.                                                       
                                                                                
  ---                                                                           
                                                                                
  # Thêm observability trước khi merge                                          
                                                                                
  Tôi muốn log lifecycle như:                                                   
                                                                                
  ```text                                                                       
  GENERATION_STARTED                                                            
  logicalTurnId=123                                                             
  responseId=abc                                                                
  nodeId=13-a                                                                   
                                                                                
  AUDIO_STARTED
  ```                                                                           

  hoặc:                                                                         
                                                                                
  ```text

  logicalTurnId=123                                                             
  responseId=abc                                                                
  elapsedMs=...                                                                 
  audioStarted=true                                                             
  interimTranscript=true                                                        
  retry=0

  GENERATION_RETRY                                                              
  logicalTurnId=123                                                             
  retry=1                                                                       
  ```                                                                           
                                                                                
  Như vậy lần sau sẽ không phải suy luận từ 275 `audio_onset` events.           
                                                                                
  ---                                                                           
                                                                                
  # Regression test bắt buộc                                                    
                                                                                
  ### 1. Normal                                                                 
                                                                                
  ```text                                                                       
  start → response.done                                                         
  ```                                                                           
                                                                                
  assert:                                                                       
                                                                                
  ```text                                                                       
  1 generation                                                                  
  1 playback                                                                    
  ```                                                                           
                                                                                
  ### 2. Stuck response                                                         
                                                                                
  Mock:                                                                         
                                                                                
  ```text                                                                       
  response.started                                                              
  audio.delta × N                                                               
  NO response.done                                                              
  ```                                                                           
                                                                                
  assert:                                                                       
                                                                                
  ```text                                                                       
  timeout                                                                       
  → cancel                                                                      
  → retry once                                                                  
  → second response completes                                                   
  ```                                                                           
                                                                                
  ### 3. Retry cannot duplicate                                                 
                                                                                
  ```text                                                                       
  attempt 1 timeout                                                             
  attempt 2 complete                                                            
  late response.done from attempt 1                                             
  ```                                                                           
                                                                                
  must **not** create second playback / second completion.                      
                                                                                
  ### 4. Caller disconnect                                                      
                                                                                
  ```text                                                                       
  generation stuck                                                              
  → caller disconnects                                                          
  ```                                                                           
                                                                                
  assert:                                                                       
                                                                                
  ```text                                                                       
  no retry after call ended                                                     
  no goodbye duplicate                                                          
  no pending generation                                                         
  ```                                                                           
                                                                                
  ### 5. 13-a exact regression                                                  
                                                                                
  ```text                                                                       
  12.5-a
  ```                                                                           

● I now have the complete picture. The watchdog design:

  Integration points in the flow engine:

  - If neither fired → Case B (no audio) → cancel + re-prompt once.

  testing. The key concern: handleGenerationTimeout calls this.speakDirective

  guard. But the re-prompt calls speakDirective which sets speechPending = true
  and arms a NEW watchdog — creating a potential retry loop if the second
  generation also stalls.

  re-prompting, so the second timeout (if it occurs) hits the if (gen.retried)

  re-prompt via speakDirective calls armGenerationWatchdog which checks if

```text                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               
silence-kick ON  → greeting kép                                                                                                                                                                                                                       
silence-kick OFF → không còn greeting kép                                                                                                                                                                                                             
                  nhưng garble / lệch turn / latency tăng mạnh                                                                                                                                                                                        
```                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      
Điều đó **chưa chứng minh silence-kick là root cause**. Ngược lại, nó có khả năng đang **che/mask một lỗi lifecycle khác**.                                                                                                                           
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      
### Cách đọc hiện tượng này                                                                                                                                                                                                                           
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      
Có thể đang xảy ra:                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      
```text                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               
Session start                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         
    │                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 
    ├── một lifecycle bug xảy ra                                                                                                                                                                                                                      
    │                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 
    ├── silence-kick tạo thêm một generation                                                                                                                                                                                                          
    │        ↓                                                                                                                                                                                                                                        
    │   generation này vô tình "ăn" lỗi                                                                                                                                                                                                               
    │                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 
    └── turn thật chạy tương đối bình thường                                                                                                                                                                                                          
```                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      
Khi bỏ kick:                                                                                                                                                                                                                                          
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      
```text                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               
Session start                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         
    │                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 
    ├── lifecycle bug vẫn xảy ra                                                                                                                                                                                                                      
    │                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                 
    └── turn thật trở thành generation đầu tiên                                                                                                                                                                                                       
              ↓                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       
       garble / latency / lệch turn                                                                                                                                                                                                                   
```                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      
Nên câu:                                                                                                                                                                                                                                              
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      
> "bỏ câu chào trùng đi nó lại làm lòi ra mấy bug đó"                                                                                                                                                                                                 
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      
là một **manh mối rất đáng giá**.

## 3. Đặc biệt kiểm tra generation đầu tiên                                                                                                                                                                                                           
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      
Report đang có một hypothesis rất hay:                                                                                                                                                                                                                
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      
> generation đầu tiên vốn dễ dính lỗi "lần đầu mở kênh", nhưng silence-kick vô tình hứng lỗi đó.

```text                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               
A. silence-kick ON                                                                                                                                                                                                                                    
   generation #1 = kick                                                                                                                                                                                                                               
   generation #2 = real user turn                                                                                                                                                                                                                     
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      
B. silence-kick OFF                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   
   generation #1 = real user turn                                                                                                                                                                                                                     
```                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      
Sau đó compare:                                                                                                                                                                                                                                       
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      
```text                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               
generation startup latency                                                                                                                                                                                                                            
response.created                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      
audio first byte                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      
response.done                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         
interrupt                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             
reconnect                                                                                                                                                                                                                                             
turn complete                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         
```                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      
Nếu lỗi luôn nằm ở:                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      
```text                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               
generation #1                                                                                                                                                                                                                                         
```                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      
bất kể nó là kick hay real turn → **silence-kick không phải root cause**.                                                                                                                                                                             
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      
Nếu lỗi chỉ xảy ra khi:                                                                                                                                                                                                                               
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      
```text                                                                                                                                                                                                                                               
generation #1 = real turn                                                                                                                                                                                                                             
```                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                   
                                                                                                                                                                                                                                                      
thì cần điều tra interaction giữa kick/session initialization và turn manager.
