  ```text                                                                       
                           ┌─────────────────────────────┐                      
                           │ Flow LLM timeout             │                     
                           │ Gemini > 8s                  │                     
                           └──────────────┬──────────────┘
  ```                                                                           

  ```text                                                                       
  Pregen greeting                                                               
        ↓
  ```                                                                           

  ```text

  ```

  Tập trung vào **speaking model + silence behavior**.                          
                                                                                
  Có 3 hướng đáng test.                                                         
                                                                                
  ### 1. Explicitly constrain silence behavior                                  
                                                                                
  Instruction cho speaking model nên có rule rất rõ:                            
                                                                                
  ```text                                                                       
  When the caller has not responded:                                            
  - Do not repeat the greeting.                                                 
  - Do not restate information already spoken.                                  
  - Do not elaborate on the previous response.                                  
  - Do not generate additional conversational filler.                           
  - Wait for the caller response.                                               
  ```                                                                           
                                                                                
  Đặc biệt câu:                                                                 
                                                                                
  > **Do not generate additional content while waiting for the caller.**        
                                                                                
  rất quan trọng.                                                               
                                                                                
  ---                                                                           
                                                                                
  ### 2. Giới hạn maximum speaking duration                                     
                                                                                
  Nếu speaking model đang:                                                      
                                                                                
  ```text                                                                       
  AS_SPEAKING                                                                   
  37s → 67s                                                                     
  ```                                                                           
                                                                                
  thì đây là red flag.                                                          
                                                                                
  Nên có guard:                                                                 
                                                                                
  ```text                                                                       
  assistant speaking                                                            
        ↓                                                                       
  max duration                                                                  
        ↓                                                                       
  stop / truncate                                                               
        ↓                                                                       
  AS_LISTENING                                                                  
  ```                                                                           
                                                                                
  Ví dụ không nhất thiết hardcode 30s ngay; benchmark:                          
                                                                                
  ```text                                                                       
  p50 speaking duration                                                         
  p95                                                                           
  p99                                                                           
  max                                                                           
  ```                                                                           
                                                                                
  rồi đặt threshold dựa trên distribution.                                      
                                                                                
  **Đây là safety guard**, không phải root-cause fix.                           
                                                                                
  ---                                                                           
                                                                                
  ### 3. Tách "silence waiting" khỏi speaking generation                        
                                                                                
  Đây là kiến trúc tốt hơn:                                                     
                                                                                
  ```text                                                                       
  Assistant response                                                            
        ↓                                                                       
  DONE                                                                          
        ↓                                                                       
  LISTENING                                                                     
        ↓                                                                       
  caller silence                                                                
        ↓                                                                       
  silence policy                                                                
        ├── short silence → wait                                                
        ├── medium silence → nudge                                              
        └── long silence → recovery/dead-call                                   
  ```                                                                           
                                                                                
  Không nên để:                                                                 
                                                                                
  ```text                                                                       
  Assistant đang generate                                                       
        ↓                                                                       
  caller im lặng                                                                
        ↓                                                                       
  model tự nghĩ:                                                                
  "maybe I should explain more..."                                              
        ↓                                                                       
  generate thêm                                                                 
  ```                                                                           
                                                                                
  Đó chính là điều trace `004` đang cho thấy.                                   
                                                                                
  ---                                                                           
                                                                                
  ## Và 3 metrics trước đó nên sửa                                              
                                                                                
  `Directive freshness` vẫn hữu ích cho flow, nhưng **không còn là metric chính 
  cho remaining bug**.                                                          
                                                                                
  Tôi sẽ thêm:                                                                  
                                                                                
  ### Speaking lifecycle                                                        
                                                                                
  ```text                                                                       
  speaking_started_at                                                           
  speaking_ended_at                                                             
  speaking_duration_ms                                                          
  generation_id                                                                 
  response_id                                                                   
  ```                                                                           
                                                                                
  ### Silence context                                                           
                                                                                
  ```text                                                                       
  caller_last_audio_at                                                          
  caller_last_final_at                                                          
  silence_duration_ms                                                           
  speaking_duration_at_silence                                                  
  ```                                                                           
                                                                                
  ### Repetition                                                                
                                                                                
  ```text                                                                       
  response_id                                                                   
  generation_id                                                                 
  node_id                                                                       
  content_hash                                                                  
  similarity_to_previous_assistant_content                                      
  ```                                                                           
                                                                                
  Sau đó trace sẽ nhìn được:                                                    
                                                                                
  ```text                                                                       
  generation=abc                                                                
  speaking=31,204ms                                                             
  caller_silence=28,900ms                                                       
  content_similarity=0.87                                                       
  ```                                                                           
                                                                                
  => gần như chắc chắn đây là **model rambling under silence**.                 
                                                                                
  ---                                                                           
                                                                                
  ## Verdict tôi sẽ chốt                                                        
                                                                                
  ```text                                                                       
  [FIXED]                                                                       
  Gemini flow-LLM timeout                                                       
  → missing transition/extraction directive                                     
                                                                                
  [FIXED]                                                                       
  OpenAI primary for transition-picker                                          
                                                                                
  [FIXED]                                                                       
  OpenAI primary for extraction                                                 
                                                                                
  [MITIGATED]                                                                   
  Original greeting flood caused by flow-LLM timeout                            
                                                                                
  [CONFIRMED]                                                                   
  Remaining greeting repetition is NOT flow re-entry                            
  NOT duplicate pregen                                                          
  NOT silence-kick root cause                                                   
                                                                                
  [CONFIRMED]                                                                   
  Remaining issue:                                                              
  speaking model rambling / self-repetition                                     
  during prolonged caller silence within one generation                         
                                                                                
  [KEEP]                                                                        
  silence-kick as recovery/dead-call safety mechanism                           
                                                                                
  [SEPARATE]                                                                    
  LKS caller nudge → no user final                                              
                                                                                
  [SEPARATE]                                                                    
  dashboard/real-call reconnect 2 → 1                                           
  ```                                                                           
                                                                                
  **Quan trọng nhất:** từ Refinement 2, tôi sẽ **bỏ hypothesis "directive       
  freshness/consumed directive gây greeting re-speak" khỏi root-cause của       
  remaining issue**. Nó vẫn đáng instrument để bảo vệ flow, nhưng trace hiện    
  tại đã chứng minh remaining failure nằm **bên trong speaking generation**,    
  không phải flow dispatch/re-entry.

  Bằng chứng từ run-001 (thí nghiệm mid-kick OFF, OpenAI gpt-realtime-2.1-mini)
  + run-005:

  ┌───────────┬─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
  │ thời điểm │                           sự kiện                           │
  ├───────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ 8.3s      │ AS_SPEAKING — pregen clip greeting bắt đầu (playout 25s,    │
  │           │ tới ~33s)                                                   │
  ├───────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ 10.7s +   │ transcript.agent.final src=sim.openai — model live-generate │
  │ 20.9s     │  greeting song song với clip                                │
  ├───────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ 32.9s     │ clip kết thúc → pregen_clip_played (bridge) + AS_LISTENING  │
  ├───────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ 33.8s     │ AS_LISTENING→AS_SPEAKING (0.83s sau clip-end)               │
  ├───────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ 55.7s     │ transcript.agent.final full greeting lần nữa                │
  ├───────────┼─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
  │ 58.2s     │ sim.openai final (rambling concat)                          │
  └───────────┴─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

  Root cause: pregen-vs-live race. Node greeting là static (isDynamic=false),
  OpenAI strategy pregenParallelRace:true. Clip được play qua playStaticClip —
  không claim speechPending, không qua speakDirective, nên không chặn được model
  cũng đang generate. Model live-generate greeting trong suốt 25s clip; khi
  clip hết nó commit generation ~0.8s sau → greeting được nói 2 lần bằng 2 đường
  (clip + live model).

  Đã loại trừ: không phải mid-kick (run-001 tắt mid-kick vẫn re-speak), không
  phải reconnect (run-001 không có caller reconnect), không phải flow re-entry
  (1 pregen, 1 node entry).

  Fix đã commit e86ef52: silence-waiting rule (platform directive, en/ja/vi) +
  wire cho cả 2 provider (Gemini 3.1 qua injectFlowInstructions; OpenAI giờ được
  inject rule vào base instructions vì trước đây rule không bao giờ tới
  OpenAI). Verify: typecheck ✓, build ✓, 706/707 tests ✓ (1 flake timing có
  sẵn). Non-flow/free-chat không đổi.

  Bước tiếp theo là chạy lại E2E (run 006) với silence-kick ON để xác nhận
  greeting chỉ nói 1 lần, rồi so latency. Chạy luôn không?

● Now I understand the run-009 failure mechanism:

  In run-009, at 127s the caller had sim.gemini_socket_drop gen 2 → reconnect
  (retryable:true). At 143s it dropped again (TimeoutError). But is_transport
  for asyncio.TimeoutError is True, and the reconnect logic sets end_call only
  if _reconnect_count > 2 (line 587) OR _reconnect_required was already set.

  Wait — run-009 showed sim.gemini_reconnecting generation:2 at 127s, then the
  second drop at 143s. After gen 2 reconnect, _reconnect_count = 2 (gen 1 + gen
  2). The 143s drop: _resume_handle present, _reconnect_required not set → it
  would try to reconnect again (gen 3). But _reconnect_count > 2 → at line 587
  it sets end_call. So after 3 reconnects, the caller gives up. The run had 3 
  socket drops in ~143s — each ~16-18s apart, matching the
  ping_interval=30s/ping_timeout=60s config OR a Gemini transport instability.

  This is the caller-side transport instability. Let me check the OpenAI-profile
  run (background) result and then address this. Let me check its status.

  Read 1 file (ctrl+o to expand)

● Now I have the complete root cause of the transcript doubling. Here's the
  mechanism:

  In applyHandlerResult for a static node:
  1. Line 2304: this.publishScriptedAgentTurn?.(resolved) — publishes the
  scripted text as a final turn (this armed+published "Am I speaking...?" as
  agent-2)
  2. Line 2305: await this.playStaticClip(...) — tries to play the pregen clip
  3. Line 2306: if clip.played → returns (good). But if clip.played is false 
  (miss), it does NOT return and falls through to line 2343: await 
  this.speakDirective(resolved, ...) — which calls publishScriptedAgentTurn
  AGAIN internally (via the conversationStatic path at line 2141) → publishes
  the SAME text as agent-3

  So when a static node's pregen clip misses (node 2-a has no clip,
  pregen_miss_fallback), the text gets published twice: once at line 2304
  (before clip) and once at line 2343 (the LLM fallback). This is the exact 
  double-publish the user saw.

  The trace confirms it perfectly:
  - Line 5866: armed (count:2) → sent agent-2
